===== NOTES =====
This probe searches only the selected candidate code regions.
A byte load plus a zero comparison is a string-processing hint, not proof.
Register tracking is local and does not model all branches or delay slots.
Signed 16-bit immediates are converted by explicit sign extension.
No process memory was written.
